== 1. Big project: 10 bands x 20 slots ==
Loaded 200 effects in 5279.95 ms; Triad latency 9284 samples
10 s of audio through 200 effects: average 10.37 ms per 256-sample block (194.4% of one core), worst 54.62 ms, peak level -30.9 dBFS
Switched on every LFO: 220 knobs now move with the song (slot mixes, band mixes, band volumes)
10 s of audio through 200 effects with every LFO on: average 10.39 ms per 256-sample block (194.7% of one core), worst 21.46 ms, peak level -15.8 dBFS
LFO cost: 0.2% more processing than the same project without LFOs

Undo with 200 effects: a snapshot takes 83.82 ms; undoing a removed effect 67.93 ms (it is loaded again, the others stay as they are); undoing a slot mix 76.42 ms; redoing both 126.72 ms
Closed the 200-effect project in 33.27 ms

Vocal chain effects: AUGraphicEQ (AU, Apple), AUNBandEQ (AU, Apple), AUParametricEQ (AU, Apple), ChowMultiTool (VST3, chowdsp), ChowMultiTool (AU, chowdsp)
Each case plays 8 s per buffer size at 48 kHz on an audio thread paced like an interface, with Triad's window open. Times are Triad's own work per block (CPU time); "late": the work took longer than the buffer lasts (a dropout on any machine); "paused by the system": the test machine (shared, virtual) stopped the thread for a moment, not Triad's work; "silenced": blocks Triad output as silence because its window was changing the bands at that moment. The project is saved every second meanwhile, like a DAW's autosave.

== 7. Recording through Triad: the extra-strong live test ==
The session: Graillon 3 (VST3, Auburn Sounds) in PRE; 3 bands at 250 Hz / 2.5 kHz (24 dB/oct) with AUGraphicEQ (AU, Apple), AUNBandEQ (AU, Apple), AUParametricEQ (AU, Apple), ChowMultiTool (VST3, chowdsp), ChowMultiTool (AU, chowdsp); every LFO on (band volumes as a 60% tremolo) and an LFO linked to a knob inside an effect; Triad's window open; the project saved every second.
(a) Monitoring while you work: a knob turned, an LFO drawn, a window opened, an undo step kept... every 150 ms. "Silenced": blocks Triad output as silence because its window was changing the bands at that moment; "dropouts": output blocks that were silent while the voice was there, for any reason; "late": Triad's work took longer than the buffer lasts.
  48.0 kHz,  32 samples (0.67 ms), 30 s, 179 actions: Triad's work average 33 us (5.0% of the time), 99.9% under 264 us; late 7 of 45000, paused by the system 7; silenced 0, dropouts 0, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 1074 samples (22.4 ms)
  48.0 kHz,  64 samples (1.33 ms), 60 s, 367 actions: Triad's work average 67 us (5.0% of the time), 99.9% under 358 us; late 2 of 45000, paused by the system 15; silenced 0, dropouts 0, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 1074 samples (22.4 ms)
  48.0 kHz, 128 samples (2.67 ms), 30 s, 178 actions: Triad's work average 256 us (9.6% of the time), 99.9% under 4279 us; late 19 of 11250, paused by the system 0; silenced 0, dropouts 0, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 1074 samples (22.4 ms)
  44.1 kHz,  64 samples (1.45 ms), 20 s, 118 actions: Triad's work average 73 us (5.0% of the time), 99.9% under 416 us; late 4 of 13781, paused by the system 1; silenced 0, dropouts 1, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 1074 samples (24.4 ms)
  96.0 kHz,  64 samples (0.67 ms), 20 s, 122 actions: Triad's work average 56 us (8.4% of the time), 99.9% under 376 us; late 9 of 30000, paused by the system 13; silenced 0, dropouts 0, not-a-number 0; Triad latency 2148 samples (22.4 ms)
(b) Layout changes while recording (48 kHz, 64 samples, one every 400 ms for 60 s): late 3 of 45000, not-a-number 0. How long each kind of change interrupted the sound:
  LIVE on or off (x12): Triad silenced 0.0 ms on average, the sound dropped out 0.0 ms on average, worst 0.0 ms
  add / remove a band (x24): Triad silenced 0.1 ms on average, the sound dropped out 0.1 ms on average, worst 1.3 ms
  all dividers: hard wall / default / soft (x15): Triad silenced 0.0 ms on average, the sound dropped out 0.0 ms on average, worst 0.0 ms
  bypass / enable an effect (x16): Triad silenced 0.2 ms on average, the sound dropped out 0.2 ms on average, worst 1.3 ms
  drag an effect to another slot (x15): Triad silenced 0.2 ms on average, the sound dropped out 0.2 ms on average, worst 1.3 ms
  load an effect into a new slot (x12): Triad silenced 0.1 ms on average, the sound dropped out 0.1 ms on average, worst 1.3 ms
  redo (x18): Triad silenced 1.9 ms on average, the sound dropped out 1.9 ms on average, worst 8.0 ms
  remove an effect (x2): Triad silenced 0.0 ms on average, the sound dropped out 0.0 ms on average, worst 0.0 ms

== 8. Pitch correction with a sung vocal: a plain track, Triad's PRE, and a copy in each band (250 Hz / 2.5 kHz) ==
The sung take: 19 held notes from A2 to G3 (110-196 Hz), each up to 40 cents off, with vibrato, breaths and "s" sounds between them; measured: 20.0 cents from the nearest note on average, wobble 12.6 cents inside a note (vibrato)
